Note that if
\Omega_{1}
were equal to
\Pi
,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
would simply be
\{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle u$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle u$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle u$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle u$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\mid u\in V\}
. So it is the natural variational space for Poisson’s problem transferred into
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Pi$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Pi$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Pi$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Pi$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
. Looking at
\chi S^{V}
as a function defined on
\Pi
, we will show by induction on
n
that:

\forall n\in\mathbb{N},\;\forall R_{\mathrm{c}}<r_{1}<r_{2,\hskip 16.38895pt}\partial_{t}^{n}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}.

Since
S^{V}\in V
, the initial case is trivial, so only the inductive step remains to prove. Let us assume the property at rank
n
and show it at rank
n+1
. We will use the method of finite differences. Letting
\mu:=\mu_{0}
on
\Omega
and
\mu:=\mu_{1}
on
\Lambda
, (
B.8
) implies:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{r@{\;=\;}ll}\mu\Delta(\chi S^{V})&f_{2}:=\chi f_{1}-2\mu\nabla\chi\cdot\nabla S^{V}-\mu\Delta\chi\cdot S^{V}&\text{ in }D,\ \forall D\in\{\Omega,\Lambda\}\\[1.42262pt]
[\mu\partial_{Y}(\chi S^{V})]_{\Gamma}&g_{2}:=\chi g_{1}&\text{ on }\Gamma\\[1.42262pt]
\chi S^{V}&0&\text{ on }\Sigma_{\Omega}\cup\Sigma_{\Lambda}\end{array}\right.

By induction hypothesis applied to
(r_{1}^{\prime},r_{2}^{\prime}):=(\frac{R_{\mathrm{c}}+r_{1}}{2},r_{1})
,
\partial_{t}^{k}\nabla S^{V}
and
\partial_{t}^{k}S^{V}
are
L^{2}
on
\{r_{1}<r<r_{2}\}
for any
k\in[\![0,n]\!]
. So (
B.9
) implies
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}_{|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}\in K^{n}_{-2}(\mathbb{R},L^{2}(0,\Theta))
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}_{|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}\in K^{n}_{-3/2}(\mathbb{R},L^{2}(-1,0))
. Similarly
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle g_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle g_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle g_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle g_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{-1/2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
. Next, we apply the change variables
(x,y)\rightsquigarrow(t,\theta)
using (

), and then
\partial_{t}^{n}
. We get that
s:=\partial_{t}^{n}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
satisfies:

{}\hskip-14.22636pt\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\;}r@{\;=\;}l@{\;\,}l@{}}-e^{-2t}\mu_{0}\Delta s&f_{\Omega}:=e^{-2t}\partial_{t}^{n}(e^{2t}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}_{|\Omega})&\text{ in }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\\[1.42262pt]

s&0&\text{ on }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\cup\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\end{array}\right.

(B.10)

with
f_{\Omega}\in K^{0}_{-2}(\Omega)
,
f_{\Lambda}\in K^{0}_{-3/2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
and
g\in K^{\infty}_{-1/2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
by induction hypothesis. The variational formulation of (
B.10
) is:
\forall\varphi\in\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu},

\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern s\cdot\nabla\varphi+\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern Let us denote
D_{\eta}\varphi(t,\theta):=\frac{\varphi(t+\eta,\theta)-\varphi(t,\theta)}{\eta}
for any
\eta\in\mathbb{R}^{*}
and any function
\varphi
. Taking
\varphi:=D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}\bar{s}
and discretely integrating by parts
D_{-\eta}
, we have:

\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}\mu_{0}|D_{\eta}\nabla s|^{2}+\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}\mu_{1}\,(D_{\eta}(e^{-t}\partial_{t}s)\cdot D_{\eta}\partial_{t}\bar{s}+e^{t}\,|D_{\eta}\partial_{\theta}s|^{2})\\
=\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}e^{2t}f_{\Omega}\cdot D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}\bar{s}+\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}\bar{s}+\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern But for any functions
\varphi,\psi
, we have
D_{\eta}(\varphi\psi)=\varphi\cdot D_{\eta}\psi+D_{\eta}\varphi\cdot\psi
. Therefore:

•

D_{\eta}(e^{-t}\partial_{t}s)\cdot D_{\eta}\partial_{t}\bar{s}=e^{-t}|D_{\eta}\partial_{t}s|^{2}+\frac{e^{-\eta}-1}{\eta}e^{-t}\partial_{t}s\cdot D_{\eta}\partial_{t}\bar{s}

•

D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}(e^{t}g)=e^{t}D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}g+2\frac{e^{\eta}-1}{\eta}e^{t}D_{\eta}g+\big(\frac{e^{\eta}-1}{\eta}\big)^{2}e^{t}g

Let us assume that
\eta
is small enough so that
\frac{|e^{\eta}-1|}{\eta}<2
and
\frac{|e^{-\eta}-1|}{\eta}<2
. Then:

, a Poincaré-type inequality gives

. Finally, using Young’s inequality

on norm products with

small enough and moving the

and

from the right-hand side to the left-hand one, we get:

\displaystyle<\infty

By definition of

, it implies that
\partial_{t}s\in\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle V$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle V$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
and completes the induction.

Step 3:
Let
K^{0}_{1/2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Pi$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Pi$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Pi$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Pi$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}):=K^{0}_{1/2}(\mathbb{R},L^{2}(-1,\Theta))
. For now we have proven that, for any
(n,i)\in(\mathbb{N}\times\{0,1\})\setminus(\{(0,0)\})
and
R_{\mathrm{c}}<r_{1}<r_{2}
,
\partial_{t}^{n}\partial_{\theta}^{i}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{0}_{1/2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Pi$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Pi$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Pi$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Pi$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})

2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\},\;\partial_{t}^{n}\partial_{\theta}^{i}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}_{|D}\in K^{0}_{1/2}(D)
. Finally, applying this to
\chi:=\chi_{\infty}
completes the proof.

\scriptstyle\Box

.

Proof of Theorem
2.4
:

For any
q\in\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{N}^{*}
, let
\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty}):=\sigma_{q}(S)
. We denote, for any
d\in\mathbb{R}
,
T_{>d}:=\mathrm{id}-T_{\leqslant d}
and

S^{\infty}_{d}:=\left\langle-R_{\partial_{Y}^{2}}\circ\partial_{x|\Lambda}^{2},\;\frac{\mu_{0}}{\mu_{1}}R_{\mathrm{N}}\circ\partial_{y|\Gamma,y=0^{+}}\right\rangle\bigg(\frac{1}{\mu_{0}}R_{\Delta}(F^{\infty}_{\Omega})+\frac{1}{\mu_{1}}R_{\partial_{Y}^{2}}(F^{\infty}_{\Lambda})+\!\fsum_{q\in\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{Z}^{*}\cap(d,\infty)}\!\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty})\,\phi_{q}\bigg)

and, for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
d_{n}:=\frac{1}{2}-n
and
S_{\mathrm{r},n}:=\chi_{\infty}S-\chi_{\infty}T_{>d_{n}}(S^{\infty}_{d_{n}})
. Let
m\in\mathbb{N}
. We will show by induction on
n
that there are coefficients
(\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty}))_{q\in-\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{N}^{*}}
s.t.:

\forall n\in\mathbb{N},\;\forall\beta>d_{n},\qquad\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m}).

For now we only know
\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty})
for
q\geqslant\frac{\pi}{\Theta}>d_{0}
, and
S_{\mathrm{r},n}
involves
\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty})
only when
q>d_{n}
, so the
n
-th inductive step involves fixing
\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty})
for all
q\in\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{Z}^{*}\cap(d_{n},d_{n-1}]
.

Initial case:

By Proposition
B.1
, there is
d\in\mathbb{R}_{-}
and
S^{V}
s.t.
S=S^{V}+\chi_{\infty}T_{\geqslant d}(S^{\infty}_{d_{0}})
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{\infty}S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{\infty}S^{V}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{\infty}S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{\infty}S^{V}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{1/2}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
. Hence

\displaystyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}

\displaystyle=\chi_{\infty}S-\chi_{\infty}T_{>d_{0}}(S^{\infty}_{d_{0}})

\displaystyle=\chi_{\infty}\big(S^{V}+\chi_{\infty}T_{\geqslant d}(S^{\infty}_{d_{0}})\big)-\chi_{\infty}T_{>d_{0}}(S^{\infty}_{d_{0}})

\displaystyle=\chi_{\infty}S^{V}+\chi_{\infty}\cdot(\chi_{\infty}-1)\cdot T_{\geqslant d}(S^{\infty}_{d_{0}})+\,\chi_{\infty}\cdot\big(T_{\geqslant d}(S^{\infty}_{d_{0}})-T_{>0}(S^{\infty}_{d_{0}})\big)

So
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{1/2}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
. But
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
is null in a vicinity of
-\infty
, so:
\forall\beta>d_{0}=\frac{1}{2},\;\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},0}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
.

Inductive step:

We assume the property at rank
n
and will show it at rank
n+1
. Let
\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}:=\chi_{\infty}S-\chi_{\infty}T_{>d_{n+1}}(S^{\infty}_{d_{n}})
and
\mathfrak{D}:=\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{Z}^{*}\cap(d_{n+1},d_{n}]
.
\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}
is a variant of
S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}
that does not involve
\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty})
for
q\in\mathfrak{D}
. We will apply Proposition
B.1
to
d:=d_{n}
and
s:=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
. To do so, we must check that:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{r@{\;\,\in\;}l}\mu_{0}\Delta\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}_{|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}&\bigcap\limits_{\beta>d_{n+1}}\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},H^{m-2}(0,\Theta))\\
\mu_{1}\,(e^{-2t}(\partial_{t}^{2}-\partial_{t})+\partial_{\theta}^{2})\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}_{|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}&\bigcap\limits_{\beta>d_{n+1}}\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},H^{m-2}(-1,0))\\
(e^{-t}\mu_{0}\partial_{\theta|\theta=0^{+}}-\mu_{1}\partial_{\theta|\theta=0^{-}})\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}&\bigcap\limits_{\beta>d_{n+1}}\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})\end{array}\right.

Let us show only the first line, the others being similar.

Since
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}=e^{-2t}\Delta\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
, it suffices to have that:
\forall\beta>d_{n+1}-2,\;\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},H^{m-2}(0,\Theta))
. And this is true because we have in
\Omega
in a vicinity of
r\rightarrow\infty
:

\displaystyle\mu_{0}\Delta\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}

\displaystyle=F-\mu_{0}\Delta\big[T_{>d_{n+1}}(S^{\infty}_{d_{n}})\big]

by definition of
\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}
, and
\mu_{0}\Delta S=F\text{ in }\Omega

\displaystyle=T_{\geqslant d_{n+1}-2}(F^{\infty}_{\Omega})+o_{\partial}(r^{d_{n+1}-2})

by hypothesis on
F

\displaystyle\ \ \ -\mu_{0}T_{>d_{n+1}-2}(\Delta S^{\infty}_{d_{n}})

by
\deg\Delta=-2
(Lemma
2.3
)

\displaystyle=(T_{\leqslant d_{n+1}-2}-T_{<d_{n+1}-2})(F^{\infty}_{\Omega})+o_{\partial}(r^{d_{n+1}-2})

by
\mu_{0}\Delta S^{\infty}_{d_{n}}=F^{\infty}_{\Omega}
(Lemma
2.4
)

Therefore, Proposition
B.1
states that there are coefficients
(c_{q})_{q\in\mathfrak{D}}
,
s_{\mathrm{r}}
and
d_{\mathrm{\min}}:=\min(\mathfrak{D})
s.t.:
\forall\beta\in(d_{n+1},d_{\mathrm{\min}}),\;s_{\mathrm{r}}\in K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}=\sum_{q\in\mathfrak{D}}c_{q}\,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}+s_{\mathrm{r}}
. Let
\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty}):=-c_{q}
for any
q\in\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{Z}^{*}\cap(d_{n+1},d_{n}]
. Then:

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\;=\;\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\tilde{S}_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}+\sum_{q\in\mathfrak{D}}\sigma_{q}(S^{\infty})\,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{\infty}\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{\infty}\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{\infty}\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{\infty}\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\;=\;s_{\mathrm{r}}+\sum_{q\in\mathfrak{D}}c_{q}\,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle(1-\chi_{\infty})\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle(1-\chi_{\infty})\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle(1-\chi_{\infty})\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle(1-\chi_{\infty})\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\;\in\!\bigcap_{\beta\in(d_{n+1},d_{\mathrm{\min}})}\!\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})

Moreover,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
is null in a vicinity of
-\infty
, so:
\forall\beta>d_{n+1},\;\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrm{r},n+1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
. This concludes the induction.

To complete the proof of the theorem, we must show that:
\forall d\in\mathbb{R},\;S=T_{\geqslant d}(S^{\infty})+o_{\partial}(\mathbf{r}^{d})
when
\mathbf{r}\rightarrow\infty
. We will do it in
\Omega
, but it works the same in
\Lambda
. Let
d\in\mathbb{R}
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
s.t.
d_{n}<d
. We have in
\Omega
:

s:=\chi_{\infty}S-\chi_{\infty}T_{\geqslant d}(S^{\infty})=S_{\mathrm{r},n}+\chi_{\infty}\cdot T_{<d}\circ T_{>d_{n}}(S^{\infty})\quad\text{ with }\quad T_{<d}\circ T_{>d_{n}}(S^{\infty})\in\sum\limits_{d^{\prime}<d}\mathcal{A}_{d^{\prime}}(\Omega).

So there is
d^{\prime}<d
s.t.:
\forall m\in\mathbb{N},\;\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle s\,$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle s\,$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle s\,$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle s\,$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{d^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R},H^{m}(0,\Theta))
. Thus for any
(i,j)\in\mathbb{N}^{2}
:

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle r^{-d^{\prime}}(r\partial_{r})^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j}s\,$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle r^{-d^{\prime}}(r\partial_{r})^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j}s\,$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle r^{-d^{\prime}}(r\partial_{r})^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j}s\,$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle r^{-d^{\prime}}(r\partial_{r})^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j}s\,$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}=e^{-d^{\prime}t}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle s\,$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle s\,$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle s\,$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle s\,$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in H^{2}(\mathbb{R},H^{2}(0,\Theta))\subset L^{\infty}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}).

So
r^{-d^{\prime}+i}\partial_{r}^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j}s
is also bounded. By definition of
o_{\partial}
(given in
2.4
), this concludes the proof.

\scriptstyle\Box

B.2
Proof of Theorem
2.4
: asymptotic behavior for far-and-layer fields-like problems

This proof is very similar to Section
B.1
. The main difference is that we look at the asymptotic expansion when
\mathbf{r}\rightarrow 0
(i.e.
t\rightarrow-\infty
) instead of
\mathbf{r}\rightarrow\infty
(i.e.
t\rightarrow+\infty
). So this time we use a truncation function
\chi_{f}\in\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\Pi)
that is equal to 1 in a vicinity of
\mathbf{r}=0
and to 0 in a vicinity of infinity. In addition, we can assume that
\chi_{f}f=0
. Moreover the Laplace transform of
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
is first defined in a left half-plane of the complex plan (instead of a right one), and then extended to the right.

Applying the change of variables
(x,y)\rightsquigarrow(t,\theta)
on the equations satisfied by
\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}
(that one can easily deduce from (
2.23
)) and then the Laplace transform yields that
s:=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}
satisfies:

{}\left\{\begin{array}[]{r@{\;=\;}ll}\mu_{0}(\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\lambda^{2})\hat{s}(\lambda,\theta)&\tilde{f}-\omega^{2}\rho_{0}\hat{s}(\lambda-2,\theta)&\text{if }\ \theta\in(0,\Theta)\\[1.42262pt]
\partial_{\theta}^{2}\hat{s}(\lambda,\theta)&\tilde{g}&\text{if }\ \theta\in(-1,0)\\[1.42262pt]
\hat{s}(\lambda,0^{+})-\hat{s}(\lambda,0^{-})&0&\\[1.42262pt]
\partial_{\theta}\hat{s}(\lambda,0^{-})&\tilde{h}&\\[1.42262pt]
\hat{s}(\lambda,\Theta)=\hat{s}(\lambda,-1)&0&\end{array}\right.

(B.12)

for some functions
\tilde{f},\tilde{g},\tilde{h}
depending on
g,h,\chi_{f}
. This system has the form of (
B.4
), so Lemma
B.1
gives the tools to solve it and to extend
\hat{s}
to the right (by steps of 2 here). This is stated in Proposition
B.2
, whose proof is very similar to Proposition
B.1
.

Proposition B.5:
Let
d\in\mathbb{R}
,
m\in\mathbb{N}\setminus\{0,1\}
,
s\in\bigcap\limits_{\beta<d}K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
,
\tilde{f}\in\!\bigcap\limits_{\beta<d+2}\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},H^{m-2}(0,\Theta))
,
\tilde{g}\in\!\bigcap\limits_{\beta<d+2}\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},H^{m-2}(-1,0))
,
\tilde{h}\in\!\bigcap\limits_{\beta<d+2}\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
and
d_{\mathrm{\max}}=\max(\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{Z}^{*}\cap[d,d+2))
. We assume:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rl}\mu_{0}\Delta s+e^{2t}\omega^{2}\rho_{0}s=\tilde{f}&\text{ in }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\\[1.42262pt]
\partial_{\theta}^{2}s=\tilde{g}&\text{ in }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Lambda$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\\[1.42262pt]
s_{|\theta=0^{+}}-s_{|\theta=0^{-}}=0&\text{ on }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\\[1.42262pt]
\mu_{1}\partial_{\theta}s_{|\theta=0^{-}}=\tilde{h}&\text{ on }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\\[1.42262pt]
s=0&\text{ on }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\cup\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\end{array}\right.

Then there is
s_{\mathrm{r}}\in\!\bigcap\limits_{d_{\mathrm{\max}}<\beta<d+2}\!K^{\infty}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
and
(c_{q})\in\mathbb{C}^{\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{Z}^{*}\cap[d,d+2)}
s.t.
\displaystyle s=\sum_{q\in\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{Z}^{*}\cap[d,d+2)}c_{q}\,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\phi_{q}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}+s_{\mathrm{r}}
.

Assumption B.6:

Replacing
\mathbf{u}
by
\chi_{\infty}\mathbf{u}
, we assume without loss of generality that
\mathbf{u}
has a compact support.

For any
q\in-\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{N}^{*}
, let
\sigma_{q}(\mathbf{u}):=\sigma_{q}(\mathbf{u}_{|\Omega})
, where
\sigma_{q}(\mathbf{u}_{|\Omega})
is set in Definition
3.1
. This quantity vanishes when
q
is small enough. Proposition
B.2
is the analogue of Proposition
B.1
.

Proposition B.7:
Let
d\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
,

\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}}:=T_{\leqslant d}\Bigg[\big\langle{-}k_{0}^{2}\,R_{\Delta}\big\rangle\bigg(\frac{1}{\mu_{1}}R_{\partial_{Y}^{2}}(g^{0})+\frac{1}{\mu_{1}}R_{\mathrm{N}}(h^{0})+\fsum_{q\in-\frac{\pi}{\Theta}\mathbb{N}^{*}}\sigma_{q}(\mathbf{u})\,\phi_{q}\bigg)\Bigg]\vskip-5.69054pt

in
\mathcal{A}(\Pi)
and
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}:=\mathbf{u}-\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}}
. There is
d\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
s.t.
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}\in H^{1}(\Omega)
and:
\forall m\in\mathbb{N},\;\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{-1}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
.

.

Proof:
We consider any
d\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
and we will fix it later. The proof has five steps: writing the equations satisfied by
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}
, and then showing that
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}
is regular in the layer, that it is
H^{1}
in
\Omega
, that it is regular in
\Omega
w.r.t
t
, and that it is regular in
\Omega
w.r.t.
\theta
. For any
r_{1},r_{2}\in\mathbb{R}
s.t.
r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{f}
, we denote
\chi\in\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
a radial function equal to 1 on
B(0,r_{1})
and 0 on
\mathbb{R}^{2}\setminus B(0,r_{2})
(
r_{1},r_{2}
are implicit in this notation).

Step 1:
Similarly to the proofs of theorems
3.1
and
3.2
and Proposition
B.1
, one can show that:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\,}r@{\;=\;}l@{\,}l@{}}(\mu_{0}\Delta+\omega^{2}\rho_{0})\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}&f_{1}:=f-(\mu_{0}\Delta+\omega^{2}\rho_{0})(\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}})&\text{ in }\Omega\\[1.42262pt]
\mu_{1}\partial_{Y}^{2}\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}&g_{1}:=g-\mu_{1}\partial_{Y}^{2}(\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}})&\text{ in }\Lambda\\[1.42262pt]
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|y=0^{+}}-\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|y=0^{-}}&0&\text{ on }\Gamma\\[2.84526pt]
\mu_{1}\partial_{Y}\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|Y=0^{-}}&h_{1}:=h-\mu_{1}\partial_{Y}(\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}})&\text{ on }\Gamma\\[2.84526pt]
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}&0&\text{ on }\Sigma_{\Omega}\cup\Sigma_{\Lambda}\end{array}\right.\mathrm{with}\ \;\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}r@{\;\in\;}l@{}}(\mu_{0}\Delta+\omega^{2}\rho_{0})\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}}&\sum\limits_{d^{\prime}>d}\!\mathcal{A}_{d^{\prime}}(\Omega)\\[1.42262pt]
\partial_{Y}^{2}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}}_{|\Lambda}-T_{\leqslant d}(g^{0})&\sum\limits_{d^{\prime}>d}\!\mathcal{A}_{d^{\prime}}(\Lambda)\\[1.42262pt]
\partial_{Y}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}}_{|Y=0^{-}}-T_{\leqslant d}(h^{0})&\sum\limits_{d^{\prime}>d}\!\mathcal{A}_{d^{\prime}}(\Gamma)\end{array}\right.

Moreover, given that
\chi f=0
for any
r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{f}
, one can check that for
d
big enough and up to decreasing
r_{f}
(without loss of generality), we have
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R},H^{m}(0,\Theta))
,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R},H^{m}(-1,0))
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
for any
m\in\mathbb{N}
.

Step 2:
Let us show that:
\forall r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{f},\forall m\in\mathbb{N},\;\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R},H^{m}(-1,0))
. By assumption, for any
x>0
,
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}
in
H^{1}
in a vicinity of
\{x\}\times[-1,0]\subset\Lambda
, so
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\{x\}\times[-1,0]}
is well-defined in
L^{2}(\{x\}\times[-1,0])
. Moreover,
g_{1}
and
h_{1}
are
o_{\partial}(x^{d})
, so they are differentiable on
(0,r_{f})\times[-1,0]
(up to decreasing
r_{f}
). Hence,
Y\in(-1,0)\mapsto\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}(x,Y)
is
\mathcal{C}^{2}
and the ODE it satisfies (see step 1) is explicitly solvable:

\forall(x,Y)\in(0,r_{f})\times[-1,0],\qquad\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}(x,Y)=\int_{-1}^{0}\big((Y-Y^{\prime})^{+}-Y-1\big)\,g_{1}(x,Y^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}Y^{\prime}+h_{1}(x)\cdot(Y+1).

Since
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi g_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R},H^{m}(-1,0))
for any
m
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi h_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
, we deduce
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Lambda}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R},H^{m}(-1,0))
.

Step 3:
Let us show that
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}\in H^{1}(\Omega)
. First, step 2 implies
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
so
\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}\in H^{1/2}(\Gamma)
. Since
\mathbf{u}\in H^{1}_{\mathrm{loc}}
and
\mathbf{u}
has a compact support by assumption
B.2
, we get
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}\in H^{1/2}(\Gamma)
. So the following system has a solution in
H^{1}(\Omega)
by Lemma
3.1
:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{r@{\;=\;}ll}\mu_{0}\Delta u^{\mathrm{v}}+\omega^{2}\rho_{0}u^{\mathrm{v}}&f_{1}&\text{ in }\Omega\\[1.42262pt]
u^{\mathrm{v}}_{|y=0^{+}}&\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|y=0^{-}}&\text{ on }\Gamma\\[2.84526pt]
u^{\mathrm{v}}&0&\text{ on }\Sigma_{\Omega}\end{array}\right.

But
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}
also satisfies these equations, so
u^{\mathrm{v}}+\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}}_{|\Omega}
satisfies the same problem as
\mathbf{u}_{|\Omega}
, and they both belong to
H^{1}(\Omega)+\chi_{0}\mathcal{A}(\Omega)
. Then by uniqueness in Theorem
3.1
,
u^{\mathrm{v}}+\chi_{f}\mathbf{u}^{\mathcal{A}}_{|\Omega}=\mathbf{u}_{|\Omega}
, i.e.
u^{\mathrm{v}}=\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}
. Thus,
\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}\in H^{1}(\Omega)
.

Step 4:
Let
v:\Omega\rightarrow\mathbb{C}
defined by
v:=\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}-v_{\mathrm{lift}}
with
v_{\mathrm{lift}}(r,\theta):=\big(1-\frac{\theta}{\Theta}\big)\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}(r)
in polar coordinates. Let
K^{0}_{\beta}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}):=K^{0}_{\beta}(\mathbb{R},L^{2}(0,\Theta))
for any
\beta\in\mathbb{R}
. We will show by induction on
n
that:

\forall n\in\mathbb{N}^{*},\;\forall r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{f},\qquad\partial_{t}^{n}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})\quad\text{ and }\quad\nabla\partial_{t}^{n}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\,v$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}).

For the initial case, we have on the one hand
\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}\in H^{1}(\Omega)
so
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
and
\nabla\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
, and on the other
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Gamma}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{1}_{0}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
so
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
and
\nabla\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
. Thus the initial case is proven and only the inductive step remains to prove. We assume the property at rank
n
and we will show it at rank
n+1
, using the method of finite differences. Step 1 implies that:

\left\{\begin{array}[]{r@{\;=\;}ll}(\mu_{0}\Delta+\omega^{2}\rho_{0})(\chi v)&f_{2}:=\chi f_{1}-\chi\,\mu_{0}\Delta v_{\mathrm{lift}}-2\mu_{0}\nabla\chi\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}-\mu_{0}\Delta\chi\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}&\text{ in }\Omega\\[1.42262pt]
\chi v&0&\text{ on }\Sigma_{\Omega}\cup\Gamma\end{array}\right.

In addition, we have
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle f_{2}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{n}_{-2}(\mathbb{R},L^{2}(0,\Theta))
since:

•

by step 1,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R},L^{2}(0,\Theta))
