\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{-2}(\mathbb{R},L^{2}(0,\Theta))
,

by step 2,

, and applying it at

gives

,

•

and by induction hypothesis applied to
(r_{1}^{\prime},r_{2}^{\prime}):=(r_{2},\frac{r_{2}+r_{f}}{2})
,

and

are

on

.

. By changing variables

s&0&\text{ on }\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Sigma_{\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\cup\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\end{array}\right.

with
f_{3}\in K^{0}_{-2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
by induction hypothesis. And the variational formulation of this is:

\forall\varphi\in K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}=0\ \mathrel{\mbox{$=\hskip-6.5pt\Rightarrow$}}\quad\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}(\mu_{0}\nabla s\cdot\nabla\varphi+\omega^{2}\rho_{0}e^{2t}s\varphi)=\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}e^{2t}f_{3}\varphi.

Let us denote
D_{\eta}\varphi(t,\theta):=\frac{\varphi(t+\eta,\theta)-\varphi(t,\theta)}{\eta}
for any
\eta\in\mathbb{R}^{*}
and any function
\varphi
. Taking
\varphi:=D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}\bar{s}
and discretely integrating by parts
D_{-\eta}
gives:

\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}(\mu_{0}|D_{\eta}\nabla s|^{2}-\omega^{2}\rho_{0}e^{2t}|s|^{2})=\int_{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}}e^{2t}f_{3}\cdot D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}\bar{s}

Then by coercivity (since
\operatorname{\operatorname{Im}}(\omega)\neq 0
), we have for any
\delta>0
:

\|D_{\eta}\nabla s\|_{L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}+\|D_{\eta}s\|_{K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}\lesssim\|f_{3}\|_{K^{0}_{-2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}\|D_{-\eta}D_{\eta}s\|_{K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu})}\lesssim{\textstyle\frac{1}{\delta}}\|f_{3}\|_{K^{0}_{-2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}+\delta\|D_{\eta}\partial_{t}s\|_{L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}

Taking
\delta
small enough and moving
\|D_{\eta}\partial_{t}s\|_{L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}
from the right-hand side to the left-hand one, we get:

\|\partial_{t}\nabla s\|_{L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}s\|_{K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}\leqslant\limsup_{\eta\rightarrow 0}\|D_{\eta}\nabla s\|_{L^{2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}\lesssim\|f_{3}\|_{K^{0}_{-2}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})}^{2}<\infty.

This completes the induction.

Step 5:
We have shown that for any
(n,i)\in\mathbb{N}\times\{0,1\}
and
r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{f}
,
\partial_{t}^{n}\partial_{\theta}^{i}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
. Since
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi v_{\mathrm{lift}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{-1}(\mathbb{R},H^{1}(0,\Theta))
, we also have
\partial_{t}^{n}\partial_{\theta}^{i}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
for these
(n,i)
.

To treat higher-order
\theta
-derivatives, we begin with the equality
(\mu_{0}\Delta+\omega^{2}\rho_{0})(\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}})=\chi f_{1}-2\mu_{0}\nabla\chi\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}-\mu_{0}\Delta\chi\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}
in
\Omega
, which follows from step 1. Applying the change of variables
(x,y)\rightsquigarrow(t,\theta)
, we get:

{}(e^{-2t}\mu_{0}\Delta+\omega^{2}\rho_{0})(\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}})=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}-2\mu_{0}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\nabla\chi\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\nabla\chi\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\nabla\chi\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\nabla\chi\cdot\nabla\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}-\mu_{0}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Delta\chi\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Delta\chi\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Delta\chi\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Delta\chi\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}.

(B.13)

Now
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi f_{1}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R},H^{m}(0,\Theta))
for any
m\in\mathbb{N}
by step 1. So deriving (
B.13
) w.r.t.
t
and
\theta
enough times gives by induction:
\forall(n,i)\in\mathbb{N}^{2},\forall r_{1}<r_{2}<r_{f},\;\partial_{t}^{n}\partial_{\theta}^{i}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}_{|\Omega}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{0}_{-1}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\Omega$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\Omega$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu})
. Combining it with step 2, we get
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-3.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\textstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}$}}}\mkern 3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu{\overbracket{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\overbracket{\mkern-2.0mu\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.0pt]{$\scriptstyle\chi\mathbf{u}^{\mathrm{v}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}$}}}\mkern 2.0mu}\in K^{\infty}_{-1}(\mathbb{R},\mathcal{H}^{m})
. Finally, we apply it to
\chi:=\chi_{\infty}
to conclude.

\scriptstyle\Box

Finally, Propositions
B.2
and
B.2
give all the ingredients to prove Theorem
2.4
. The proof is very similar to the one of Theorem
2.4
on page
B.1
, so we do not go into details again.
